\"\"\"\n    runs = list(p_el.findall(\"hp:run\", NS))\n    if not runs:\n        run = ET.Element(qname(\"run\"))\n        run.set(\"charPrIDRef\", \"0\")\n        p_el.append(run)\n        runs =
